}\n\n    size_t localRows() const {\n        size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank), p = static_cast<size_t>(nprocs);\n        return r >= numTriangles ? 0 : (numTriangles - r + p - 1) / p;\n    }\n    size_t paddedRows() const {\n        size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(nprocs);\n        return (numTriangles + p - 1) / p;\n    }\n    size_t globalRow(size_t k) const {\n        return static_cast<size_t>(rank) + k * static_cast<size_t>(nprocs);\n    }\n};\n\n// Gather per-rank (padded) local row values into a global array indexed by row.\nvoid allgatherRows(const SimulationState& state, const std::vector<val_t>& local, val_t* out) {\n    size_t m = state.paddedRows();\n    size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(state.nprocs);\n    std::vector<val_t> recv(m * p);\n    MPI_Allgather(local.data(), static_cast<int>(m), MPI_FLOAT,\n                  recv.data(), static_cast<int>(m), MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < p; ++r) {\n        for (size_t k = 0, i = r; i < state.numTriangles; ++k, i += p) {\n            out[i] = recv[r * m + k];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Initialization\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    // Generate mesh (replicated on every rank; cheap compared to the rest)\n    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\n    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\n    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\n    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\n    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\n\n    if (state.rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles);\n        printf(\"Building octree...\\n\");\n    }\n\n    // Build octree for spatial acceleration\n    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\n\n    // Initialize areas\n    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n    }\n\n    // Initialize reflectivity\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n\n    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n\n    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\n    size_t timeOn = 0;\n    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;\n    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {\n        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Precomputation Phase\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Form factors and time delays for the locally owned rows.\n//\n// The sequential code draws all random numbers from one stream in (i, j)\n// row-major order; every non-culled pair consumes exactly RANDS_PER_PAIR\n// numbers. We reproduce the identical stream by computing each row's offset\n// (global exclusive prefix sum of per-row consumption) and skipping ahead.\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n        printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n    }\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t nloc = state.localRows();\n\n    // Per-row random consumption (computed for owned rows, then shared)\n    std::vector<uint64_t> rowRands(n, 0);\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < nloc; ++k) {\n        size_t i = state.globalRow(k);\n        uint64_t cnt = 0;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            if (!isCulled(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j])) ++cnt;\n        }\n        rowRands[i] = cnt * RANDS_PER_PAIR;\n    }\n    MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, rowRands.data(), static_cast<int>(n),\n                  MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    std::vector<uint64_t> rowStart(n);\n    uint64_t acc = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        rowStart[i] = acc;\n        acc += rowRands[i];\n    }\n\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n    uint64_t streamPos = 0;\n\n    state.rowPtr.assign(1, 0);\n    state.rowPtr.reserve(nloc + 1);\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < nloc; ++k) {\n        size_t i = state.globalRow(k);\n        rng.skip(rowStart[i] - streamPos);\n        streamPos = rowStart[i];\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            val_t kij = computeKij(i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n            if (kij > EPSILON) ++state.localNonZeroKij;\n            if (kij <= ZERO) continue;\n            state.colJ.push_back(static_cast<idx_t>(j));\n            state.colTau.push_back(computeTau(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]));\n            state.colW.push_back(std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE));\n        }\n        streamPos += rowRands[i];\n        state.rowPtr.push_back(state.colJ.size());\n\n        if (state.rank == 0 && ((k + 1) % 100 == 0 || k + 1 == nloc)) {\n            size_t done = std::min(n, (k + 1) * static_cast<size_t>(state.nprocs));\n            printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles (rank 0)\\n\", done, n);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t nloc = state.localRows();\n    std::vector<val_t> localB(state.paddedRows(), ZERO);\n\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n        const int ti = static_cast<int>(t);\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < nloc; ++k) {\n            size_t i = state.globalRow(k);\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t e = state.rowPtr[k]; e < state.rowPtr[k + 1]; ++e) {\n                int tauij = state.colTau[e];\n\n                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n                if (ti < tauij) continue;\n\n                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\n                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\n                val_t radJ = state.radB[srcTime * n + state.colJ[e]];\n                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\n                sumB += state.colW[e] * radJ;\n            }\n\n            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\n            localB[k] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];\n        }\n\n        // Exchange this timestep's radiosity (tau >= 1, so later steps need it)\n        allgatherRows(state, localB, &state.radB[t * n]);\n\n        if (state.rank == 0 && ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps)) {\n            printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t T = state.numTimesteps;\n    const size_t nloc = state.localRows();\n    std::vector<val_t> localD(state.paddedRows(), ZERO);\n\n    // Contiguous copy of the source signal\n    std::vector<val_t> src(T), col(T);\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) src[t] = state.radB[t * n + state.sourceIndex];\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < nloc; ++k) {\n        size_t i = state.globalRow(k);\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) col[t] = state.radB[t * n + i];\n\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n        int bestT = 0;\n\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < T; ++tt) {\n                sum += src[tt - t] * col[tt];\n            }\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n                maxCorr = sum;\n                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\n            }\n        }\n\n        localD[k] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\n    }\n\n    allgatherRows(state, localD, state.distances.data());\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Validation (run on rank 0; all data is replicated except Kij statistics)\n// ============================================================================\n\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state, uint64_t nonZeroKijTotal) {\n    printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\n    int nonZeroCount = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t d = state.distances[i];\n        if (d < 0) {\n            allNonNegative = false;\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n        }\n        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n            return false;\n        }\n        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\n        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\n        sumDist += d;\n        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\n\", minDist, maxDist);\n    printf(\"  Average distance: %.4f\\n\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));\n    printf(\"  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\\n\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Check source distance is zero or very small\n    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];\n    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\n\", srcDist);\n    }\n\n    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\n    int receivedEnergy = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {\n                receivedEnergy++;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\n\", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);\n\n    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)\n    int nonZeroKij = static_cast<int>(nonZeroKijTotal);\n    printf(\"  Non-zero form factors: %d/%zu (%.2f%%)\\n\",\n           nonZeroKij, state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,\n           100.0f * nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));\n\n    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!allNonNegative) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Hash for Verification\n// ============================================================================\n\nuint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);\n        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count\n// ============================================================================\n\nint getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {\n    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision\n    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480\n    int subdivisions = 0;\n    int triangles = 20;\n    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {\n        subdivisions++;\n        triangles *= 4;\n    }\n    return subdivisions;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Main\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\\n\");\n    printf(\"               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\\n\");\n    printf(\"               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -o           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint run(int argc, char** argv, int rank, int nprocs) {\n    int targetTriangles = 320;\n    int timesteps = 50;\n    int sourceIdx = 0;\n    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments (identical on all ranks)\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-o\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    int subdivisions =    printf(\"Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\\n\", targetTriangles, subdivisions);\n        printf(\"Timesteps: %d\\n\", timesteps);\n        printf(\"Source triangle: %d\\n\",     printf(\"\\n\");\n    }\n\n    // Initialize\n    SimulationState state;\n    state.rank = rank;\n    state.nprocs = nprocs;\n    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),\n                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);\n\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"\\n\");\n\n    auto elapsedMs = [](std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::time_point a) {\n        auto b = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n        return static_cast<long>(std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(b - a).count());\n    };\n\n    // Precomputation\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto startPre =  int status = 0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Total computation time: %ld ms\\n\", totalTime);\n\n        // Performance metrics\n        size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n        size_t t = state.numTimesteps;\n        double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n * n);\n        double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);\n        double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);\n\n        printf(\"\\nPerformance:\\n\");\n        printf(\"  Triangles: %zu\\n\", n);\n        printf(\"  Timesteps: %zu\\n\", t);\n        printf(\"  Form factor computations: %.2e\\n\", kijOps);\n        printf(\"  Simulation operations: %.2e\\n\", simOps);\n        printf(\"  Distance computations: %.2e\\n\", distOps);\n        printf(\"  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\\n\", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);\n\n        // Memory usage (of the equivalent dense formulation)\n        size_t memKij = n * n * sizeof(val_t);\n        size_t memTau = n * n * sizeof(int);\n        size_t memRad = 2 * t * n * sizeof(val_t);\n        size_t totalMem = memKij + memTau + memRad;\n        printf(\"  Memory usage: %.2f MB\\n\", totalMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));\n\n        // Hash\n        uint64_t hash = computeHash(state);\n        printf(\"  Result hash: %016lX\\n\", hash);\n        printf(\"\\n\");\n\n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            // Convert distances to double for output\n            std::vector<double> distData(state.distances.begin(), state.distances.end());\n            print_results(distData, \"Distances\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            if (!validateResults(state, nonZeroKijTotal)) {\n                status = 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    return status;\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    int rank = 0, nprocs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nprocs);\n\n    int rc = run(argc, argv, rank, nprocs);\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return rc;\n}\nEOF\ncat head.tmp tail.tmp Every rank holds the (cheap) mesh,\n// areas, octree and the complete radiosity history; the large N x N Kij/Tau\n// matrices exist only as a sparse (CSR) slice for the locally owned rows.\nstruct SimulationState {\n    int rank = 0;\n    int nprocs = 1;\n\n    size_t numTriangles = 0;\n    size_t numTimesteps = 0;\n\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n    std::vector<val_t> areas;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix, replicated)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source (replicated)\n\n    // Local rows: sparse Kij (stored as min(Kij*area_j, 1)) and Tau, ascending j\n    std::vector<size_t> rowPtr;\n    std::vector<idx_t> colJ;\n    std::vector<int> colTau;\n    std::vector<val_t> colW;\n    uint64_t localNonZeroKij = 0;   // entries with Kij > EPSILON\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n\n    size_t sourceIndex = 0;\n\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n\n    size_t localRows() const {\n        size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank), p = static_cast<size_t>(nprocs);\n        return r >= numTriangles ? 0 : (numTriangles - r + p - 1) / p;\n    }\n    size_t paddedRows() const {\n        size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(nprocs);\n        return (numTriangles + p - 1) / p;\n    }\n    size_t globalRow(size_t k) const {\n        return static_cast<size_t>(rank) + k * static_cast<size_t>(nprocs);\n    }\n};\n\n// Gather per-rank (padded) local row values into a global array indexed by row.\nvoid allgatherRows(const SimulationState& state, const std::vector<val_t>& local, val_t* out) {\n    size_t m = state.paddedRows();\n    size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(state.nprocs);\n    std::vector<val_t> recv(m * p);\n    MPI_Allgather(local.data(), static_cast<int>(m), MPI_FLOAT,\n                  recv.data(), static_cast<int>(m), MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < p; ++r) {\n        for (size_t k = 0, i = r; i < state.numTriangles; ++k, i += p) {\n            out[i] = recv[r * m + k];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Initialization\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    // Generate mesh (replicated on every rank; cheap compared to the rest)\n    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\n    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\n    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\n    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\n    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\n\n    if (state.rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles);\n        printf(\"Building octree...\\n\");\n    }\n\n    // Build octree for spatial acceleration\n    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\n\n    // Initialize areas\n    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n    }\n\n    // Initialize reflectivity\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n\n    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n\n    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\n    size_t timeOn = 0;\n    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;\n    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {\n        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Precomputation Phase\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Form factors and time delays for the locally owned rows.\n//\n// The sequential code draws all random numbers from one stream in (i, j)\n// row-major order; every non-culled pair consumes exactly RANDS_PER_PAIR\n// numbers. We reproduce the identical stream by computing each row's offset\n// (global exclusive prefix sum of per-row consumption) and skipping ahead.\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n        printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n    }\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t nloc = state.localRows();\n\n    // Per-row random consumption (computed for owned rows, then shared)\n    std::vector<uint64_t> rowRands(n, 0);\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < nloc; ++k) {\n        size_t i = state.globalRow(k);\n        uint64_t cnt = 0;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            if (!isCulled(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j])) ++cnt;\n        }\n        rowRands[i] = cnt * RANDS_PER_PAIR;\n    }\n    MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, rowRands.data(), static_cast<int>(n),\n                  MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    std::vector<uint64_t> rowStart(n);\n    uint64_t acc = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        rowStart[i] = acc;\n        acc += rowRands[i];\n    }\n\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n    uint64_t streamPos = 0;\n\n    state.rowPtr.assign(1, 0);\n    state.rowPtr.reserve(nloc + 1);\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < nloc; ++k) {\n        size_t i = state.globalRow(k);\n        rng.skip(rowStart[i] - streamPos);\n        streamPos = rowStart[i];\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            val_t kij = computeKij(i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n            if (kij > EPSILON) ++state.localNonZeroKij;\n            if (kij <= ZERO) continue;\n            state.colJ.push_back(static_cast<idx_t>(j));\n            state.colTau.push_back(computeTau(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]));\n            state.colW.push_back(std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE));\n        }\n        streamPos += rowRands[i];\n        state.rowPtr.push_back(state.colJ.size());\n\n        if (state.rank == 0 && ((k + 1) % 100 == 0 || k + 1 == nloc)) {\n            size_t done = std::min(n, (k + 1) * static_cast<size_t>(state.nprocs));\n            printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles (rank 0)\\n\", done, n);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t nloc = state.localRows();\n    std::vector<val_t> localB(state.paddedRows(), ZERO);\n\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n        const int ti = static_cast<int>(t);\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < nloc; ++k) {\n            size_t i = state.globalRow(k);\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t e = state.rowPtr[k]; e < state.rowPtr[k + 1]; ++e) {\n                int tauij = state.colTau[e];\n\n                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n                if (ti < tauij) continue;\n\n                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\n                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\n                val_t radJ = state.radB[srcTime * n + state.colJ[e]];\n                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\n                sumB += state.colW[e] * radJ;\n            }\n\n            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\n            localB[k] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];\n        }\n\n        // Exchange this timestep's radiosity (tau >= 1, so later steps need it)\n        allgatherRows(state, localB, &state.radB[t * n]);\n\n        if (state.rank == 0 && ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps)) {\n            printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t T = state.numTimesteps;\n    const size_t nloc = state.localRows();\n    std::vector<val_t> localD(state.paddedRows(), ZERO);\n\n    // Contiguous copy of the source signal\n    std::vector<val_t> src(T), col(T);\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) src[t] = state.radB[t * n + state.sourceIndex];\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < nloc; ++k) {\n        size_t i = state.globalRow(k);\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) col[t] = state.radB[t * n + i];\n\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n        int bestT = 0;\n\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < T; ++tt) {\n                sum += src[tt - t] * col[tt];\n            }\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n                maxCorr = sum;\n                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\n            }\n        }\n\n        localD[k] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\n    }\n\n    allgatherRows(state, localD, state.distances.data());\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Validation (run on rank 0; all data is replicated except Kij statistics)\n// ============================================================================\n\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state, uint64_t nonZeroKijTotal) {\n    printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\n    int nonZeroCount = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t d = state.distances[i];\n        if (d < 0) {\n            allNonNegative = false;\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n        }\n        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n            return false;\n        }\n        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\n        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\n        sumDist += d;\n        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\n\", minDist, maxDist);\n    printf(\"  Average distance: %.4f\\n\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));\n    printf(\"  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\\n\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Check source distance is zero or very small\n    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];\n    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\n\", srcDist);\n    }\n\n    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\n    int receivedEnergy = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {\n                receivedEnergy++;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\n\", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);\n\n    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)\n    int nonZeroKij = static_cast<int>(nonZeroKijTotal);\n    printf(\"  Non-zero form factors: %d/%zu (%.2f%%)\\n\",\n           nonZeroKij, state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,\n           100.0f * nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));\n\n    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!allNonNegative) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Hash for Verification\n// ============================================================================\n\nuint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);\n        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count\n// ============================================================================\n\nint getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {\n    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision\n    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480\n    int subdivisions = 0;\n    int triangles = 20;\n    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {\n        subdivisions++;\n        triangles *= 4;\n    }\n    return subdivisions;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Main\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\\n\");\n    printf(\"               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\\n\");\n    printf(\"               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -o           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint run(int argc, char** argv, int rank, int nprocs) {\n    int targetTriangles = 320;\n    int timesteps = 50;\n    int sourceIdx = 0;\n    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments (identical on all ranks)\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-o\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    int subdivisions =    printf(\"Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\\n\", targetTriangles, subdivisions);\n        printf(\"Timesteps: %d\\n\", timesteps);\n        printf(\"Source triangle: %d\\n\", sourceIdx);\n        printf(\"Reflectivity: %.2f\\n\", reflectivity);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", nprocs);\n        printf(\"\\n\");\n    }\n\n    // Initialize\n    SimulationState state;\n    state.rank = rank;\n    state.nprocs = nprocs;\n    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, [](std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::time_point a) {\n        auto b = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n        return static_cast<long>(std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(b - a).count());\n    };\n\n    // Precomputation\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto startPre =  int status = 0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Total computation time: %ld ms\\n\", totalTime);\n\n        // Performance metrics\n        size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n        size_t t = state.numTimesteps;\n        double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n * n);\n        double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);\n        double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);\n\n        printf(\"\\nPerformance:\\n\");\n        printf(\"  Triangles: %zu\\n\", n);\n        printf(\"  Timesteps: %zu\\n\", t);\n        printf(\"  Form factor computations: %.2e\\n\", kijOps);\n        printf(\"  Simulation operations: %.2e\\n\", simOps);\n        printf(\"  Distance computations: %.2e\\n\", distOps);\n        printf(\"  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\\n\", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);\n\n        // Memory usage (of the equivalent dense formulation)\n        size_t memKij = n * n * sizeof(val_t);\n        size_t memTau = n * n * sizeof(int);\n        size_t memRad = 2 * t * n * sizeof(val_t);\n        size_t totalMem = memKij + memTau + memRad;\n        printf(\"  Memory usage: %.2f MB\\n\", totalMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));\n\n        // Hash\n        uint64_t hash = computeHash(state);\n        printf(\"  Result hash: %016lX\\n\", hash);\n        printf(\"\\n\");\n\n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            // Convert distances to double for output\n            std::vector<double> distData(state.distances.begin(), state.distances.end());\n            print_results(distData, \"Distances\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            if (!validateResults(state, nonZeroKijTotal)) {\n                status = 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    return status;\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    int rank = 0, nprocs = 1;\n Every rank holds the (cheap) mesh,\n// areas, octree and the complete radiosity history; the large N x N Kij/Tau\n// matrices exist only as a sparse (CSR) slice for the locally owned rows.\nstruct SimulationState {\n    int rank = 0;\n    int nprocs = 1;\n\n    size_t numTriangles = 0;\n    size_t numTimesteps = 0;\n\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle\n    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)\n    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix, replicated)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source (replicated)\n\n    // Local rows: sparse Kij (stored as min(Kij*area_j, 1)) and Tau, ascending j\n    std::vector<size_t> rowPtr;\n    std::vector<idx_t> colJ;\n    std::vector<int> colTau;\n    std::vector<val_t> colW;\n    uint64_t localNonZeroKij = 0;   // entries with Kij > EPSILON\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n\n    size_t sourceIndex = 0;\n\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n\n    size_t localRows() const {\n        size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank), p = static_cast<size_t>(nprocs);\n        return r >= numTriangles ? 0 : (numTriangles - r + p - 1) / p;\n    }\n    size_t paddedRows() const {\n        size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(nprocs);\n        return (numTriangles + p - 1) / p;\n    }\n    size_t globalRow(size_t k) const {\n        return static_cast<size_t>(rank) + k * static_cast<size_t>(nprocs);\n    }\n};\n\n// Gather per-rank (padded) local row values into a global array indexed by row.\nvoid allgatherRows(const SimulationState& state, const std::vector<val_t>& local, val_t* out) {\n    size_t m = state.paddedRows();\n    size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(state.nprocs);\n    std::vector<val_t> recv(m * p);\n    MPI_Allgather(local.data(), static_cast<int>(m), MPI_FLOAT,\n                  recv.data(), static_cast<int>(m), MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < p; ++r) {\n        for (size_t k = 0, i = r; i < state.numTriangles; ++k, i += p) {\n            out[i] = recv[r * m + k];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Initialization\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    // Generate mesh (replicated on every rank; cheap compared to the rest)\n    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\n    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\n    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\n    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\n    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\n\n    if (state.rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles);\n        printf(\"Building octree...\\n\");\n    }\n\n    // Build octree for spatial acceleration\n    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\n\n    // Initialize areas\n    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n    }\n\n    // Initialize reflectivity\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n\n    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n\n    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\n    size_t timeOn = 0;\n    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;\n    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {\n        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Precomputation Phase\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Form factors and time delays for the locally owned rows.\n//\n// The sequential code draws all random numbers from one stream in (i, j)\n// row-major order; every non-culled pair consumes exactly RANDS_PER_PAIR\n// numbers. We reproduce the identical stream by computing each row's offset\n// (global exclusive prefix sum of per-row consumption) and skipping ahead.\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n        printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n    }\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t nloc = state.localRows();\n\n    // Per-row random consumption (computed for owned rows, then shared)\n    std::vector<uint64_t> rowRands(n, 0);\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < nloc; ++k) {\n        size_t i = state.globalRow(k);\n        uint64_t cnt = 0;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            if (!isCulled(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j])) ++cnt;\n        }\n        rowRands[i] = cnt * RANDS_PER_PAIR;\n    }\n    MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, rowRands.data(), static_cast<int>(n),\n                  MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    std::vector<uint64_t> rowStart(n);\n    uint64_t acc = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        rowStart[i] = acc;\n        acc += rowRands[i];\n    }\n\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n    uint64_t streamPos = 0;\n\n    state.rowPtr.assign(1, 0);\n    state.rowPtr.reserve(nloc + 1);\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < nloc; ++k) {\n        size_t i = state.globalRow(k);\n        rng.skip(rowStart[i] - streamPos);\n        streamPos = rowStart[i];\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            val_t kij = computeKij(i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n            if (kij > EPSILON) ++state.localNonZeroKij;\n            if (kij <= ZERO) continue;\n            state.colJ.push_back(static_cast<idx_t>(j));\n            state.colTau.push_back(computeTau(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]));\n            state.colW.push_back(std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE));\n        }\n        streamPos += rowRands[i];\n        state.rowPtr.push_back(state.colJ.size());\n\n        if (state.rank == 0 && ((k + 1) % 100 == 0 || k + 1 == nloc)) {\n            size_t done = std::min(n, (k + 1) * static_cast<size_t>(state.nprocs));\n            printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles (rank 0)\\n\", done, n);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t nloc = state.localRows();\n    std::vector<val_t> localB(state.paddedRows(), ZERO);\n\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n        const int ti = static_cast<int>(t);\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < nloc; ++k) {\n            size_t i = state.globalRow(k);\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t e = state.rowPtr[k]; e < state.rowPtr[k + 1]; ++e) {\n                int tauij = state.colTau[e];\n\n                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n                if (ti < tauij) continue;\n\n                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\n                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\n                val_t radJ = state.radB[srcTime * n + state.colJ[e]];\n                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\n                sumB += state.colW[e] * radJ;\n            }\n\n            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\n            localB[k] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];\n        }\n\n        // Exchange this timestep's radiosity (tau >= 1, so later steps need it)\n        allgatherRows(state, localB, &state.radB[t * n]);\n\n        if (state.rank == 0 && ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps)) {\n            printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t T = state.numTimesteps;\n    const size_t nloc = state.localRows();\n    std::vector<val_t> localD(state.paddedRows(), ZERO);\n\n    // Contiguous copy of the source signal\n    std::vector<val_t> src(T), col(T);\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) src[t] = state.radB[t * n + state.sourceIndex];\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < nloc; ++k) {\n        size_t i = state.globalRow(k);\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) col[t] = state.radB[t * n + i];\n\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n        int bestT = 0;\n\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < T; ++tt) {\n                sum += src[tt - t] * col[tt];\n            }\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n                maxCorr = sum;\n                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\n            }\n        }\n\n        localD[k] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\n    }\n\n    allgatherRows(state, localD, state.distances.data());\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Validation (run on rank 0; all data is replicated except Kij statistics)\n// ============================================================================\n\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state, uint64_t nonZeroKijTotal) {\n    printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\n    int nonZeroCount = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t d = state.distances[i];\n        if (d < 0) {\n            allNonNegative = false;\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n        }\n        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n            return false;\n        }\n        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\n        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\n        sumDist += d;\n        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\n\", minDist, maxDist);\n    printf(\"  Average distance: %.4f\\n\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));\n    printf(\"  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\\n\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Check source distance is zero or very small\n    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];\n    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\n\", srcDist);\n    }\n\n    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\n    int receivedEnergy = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {\n                receivedEnergy++;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\n\", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);\n\n    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)\n    int nonZeroKij = static_cast<int>(nonZeroKijTotal);\n    printf(\"  Non-zero form factors: %d/%zu (%.2f%%)\\n\",\n           nonZeroKij, state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,\n           100.0f * nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));\n\n    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!allNonNegative) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Hash for Verification\n// ============================================================================\n\nuint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);\n        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count\n// ============================================================================\n\nint getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {\n    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision\n    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480\n    int subdivisions = 0;\n    int triangles = 20;\n    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {\n        subdivisions++;\n        triangles *= 4;\n    }\n    return subdivisions;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Main\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\\n\");\n    printf(\"               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\\n\");\n    printf(\"               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint run(int argc, char** argv, int rank, int nprocs) {\n    int targetTriangles = 320;\n    int timesteps = 50;\n    int sourceIdx = 0;\n    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments (identical on all ranks)\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0 && i + 1 < == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if [](std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::time_point a) {\n        auto b = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n        return printf(\"  Simulation operations: %.2e\\n\", simOps);\n        printf(\"  Distance computations: %.2e\\n\", distOps);\n        printf(\"  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\\n\", The validation checks (`-v`) also passed at 320 triangles with the same form-factor counts.\n\nThe 3-rank and 8-rank runs used `--oversubscribe` on a 256-core machine. Scaling is close to linear at these sizes.\n\n- **Work split:** Each rank owns every P-th triangle row. It computes the form factors and time delays for those rows only. Nothing is stored for rows it doesn't own, and the dense N×N matrices are replaced by a sparse per-rank table of the nonzero entries.\n- **Same random numbers as the original:** The original draws every random number from one sequential stream, so it can't simply be split by row. Each pair of triangles uses a fixed number of random numbers, so I count them per row and share the counts across ranks. Each rank then skips ahead in its own copy of the generator to the right position, which is why the hashes match exactly. The generator is a bit-identical `mt19937` with a fast skip.\n- **Simulation:** Each timestep, ranks compute their rows and then share the result with `MPI_Allgather`. Summation order is unchanged.\n- **Distances:** The cross-correlation is split by row, then gathered.\n- **Output:** Only rank 0 prints, including the validation and `-o` output. The exit status is broadcast to all ranks. I added one line to the header showing the MPI rank count.\n- **Build:** `CMakeLists.txt` now requires MPI (`find_package(MPI REQUIRED)`) and links `MPI::MPI_CXX`. The executable is still `roomsim`.\n\nEach rank still builds its own copy of the mesh and octree. Each rank also has to step its generator through the whole random stream.
